# Armas y aviones en su versión más moderna: 230 pedidos (24 de octubre de 2026)
# La imagen que ya tenés de cada arma queda como el nivel I (la vieja); esta es el último nivel (la moderna).
# Los niveles del medio usan la más cercana. Cada pedido trae el nombre de archivo exacto.

## 1
ASSET_REQUEST
type: weapon_icon
id: arma_APF_amphibious_2
filename: arma_APF_amphibious_2.png
size: 600x400 (o más grande con la misma proporción)
style: Hearts of Iron IV style, retrofuturist year 2100, painterly propaganda-poster look, strong silhouette readable at small size, no text, no letters, no watermark; African peoples' federation: village councils, savanna and rising industry, kente-like patterns, earth red, yellow and green
description: GENERATION 2 OF 2 of the Kiboko Drone (Dron Kiboko): an amphibious armoured vehicle coming out of the water. It is the most advanced, final version of the same weapon line, with the SAME faction design language as generation 1 (arma_APF_amphibious.png): An amphibious vehicle like a hippo (kiboko): a fat rounded grey-pink hull wallowing half-submerged in a river, small eyes and ears on top as sensors, a wide mouth-shaped front ramp, water jets behind. Show the technological progress so it is obviously far newer than generation 1: the second generation is bigger and better armoured, with water jets and a missile turret. Keep the faction materials, colours and silhouette family; only the technology level changes, like Infantry Equipment I, II, III in Hearts of Iron IV. FACTION DESIGN PHILOSOPHY - RUGGED, REPAIRABLE, PEOPLE'S TECHNOLOGY. The African Peoples' Federation builds tough, simple, modern machines that any village council can maintain: open frames, big wheels, huge ground clearance, exposed easy-to-replace parts, civilian and agricultural origins (tractor, truck and mining machinery DNA), solar and biofuel power, lots of external storage, water jerrycans and spare tyres. Armour is painted with bright kente-like geometric bands; mobility and range matter more than heavy armour. Palette: earth red, savanna ochre, deep green, bright yellow and black geometric patterns. Single object, three-quarter or side view, centred, fills most of the frame, transparent background, no text, no letters, no numbers.
transparent_background: true

It is the most advanced, final version of the same weapon line, with the SAME faction design language as generation 1 (arma_ASC_motorized.png): An autonomous electric transport pod on six small wheels: a smooth white capsule with no windshield and no driver, a red band with sensor eyes all around, sliding side doors showing rows of identical robot soldiers folded inside. Show the technological progress so it is obviously far newer than generation 1: the second generation is armoured, with a remote weapon turret and better tyres. Keep the faction materials, colours and silhouette family; only the technology level changes, like Infantry Equipment I, II, III in Hearts of Iron IV. FACTION DESIGN PHILOSOPHY - NO HUMANS NEEDED. The Automated Socialist Commune builds crewless machines designed by planning algorithms: everything is a robot or a drone, with no seats, no hatches, no windows, no handles for human hands. Shapes are pure constructivist geometry: hexagonal and triangular prisms, flat panels, exposed modular blocks that clip together like construction toys, rows of identical sensor eyes, visible serial numbers as geometric markings (no letters), red indicator lights. White ceramic armour with red structural frames and black sensor glass, like a clean factory robot that went to war. Multiple legs or tracks are common; nothing looks handcrafted, everything looks printed in a series of thousands. Single object, three-quarter or side view, centred, fills most of the frame, transparent background, no text, no letters, no numbers.

The Ecofascist Empire does not manufacture weapons, it cultivates them in bio-forges: every object is partly alive, made of "bio-steel", a dark green-bronze material that looks like lacquered hardwood fused with insect chitin, with visible growth rings, veins and knots. No straight lines, no bolts, no welds: surfaces flow like roots and shells, joints are sinew-like tendons, cooling is done by living leaves or gills, and glowing amber sap runs in translucent veins. Metal appears only as small gold imperial laurel inlays. Silhouettes are animal-like (the weapons are named after South American fauna and really resemble them). Palette: deep forest green, bark brown, chitin bronze, amber sap glow, small gold accents. description: GENERATION 4 OF 4 of the Sovereign Current-class Capital Ship (Buque Capital clase Corriente Soberana): a huge capital ship with railguns, side view. It is the most advanced, final version of the same weapon line, with the SAME faction design language as generation 1 (arma_HSN_capital.png): A huge capital ship converted from an ultra-large container ship: kilometre-long hull with mountains of colourful containers that open to reveal railguns, a tall white bridge tower, gantry cranes, a brass compass-rose emblem. Show the technological progress so it is obviously far newer than generation 1: each generation is longer and sleeker, with more missile cells and radar masts, then stealth angled hull shapes, finally railguns and laser turrets. Keep the faction materials, colours and silhouette family; only the technology level changes, like Infantry Equipment I, II, III in Hearts of Iron IV. FACTION DESIGN PHILOSOPHY - EVERYTHING COMES FROM THE SEA TRADE. The High Seas Market Nation converts merchant-marine technology into weapons: hulls are shipping containers and boat hulls, mounts are dock cranes and winches, armour is riveted ship plate with hazard stripes and anti-fouling red paint below the "waterline", and almost every land vehicle is amphibious with propellers, rudders, bilge pumps and life-raft canisters. Nautical details everywhere: portholes, cleats, coiled ropes, anchors, signal flags without letters, compass roses, brass fittings, container corner castings. Palette: petrol teal, rust orange, container blue and red, brass, white hull numbers as shapes. Single object, three-quarter or side view, centred, fills most of the frame, transparent background, no text, no letters, no numbers.

The Neo-Andean Sun Kingdom builds light, high-altitude, sun-powered weapons: every vehicle and gun carries folding golden solar panels shaped like sun rays or condor feathers, frames are lightweight alloy over carved stone-like ceramic plates with Inca stepped (chakana) geometry, and machines are tall, narrow and long-legged to climb Andean slopes. Woven textile patterns (aguayo stripes) decorate armour, turquoise crystal lenses replace glass, and many weapons use gravity or wind (gliders, slings, rails). Palette: gold, turquoise, terracotta red, alpaca white and stone grey. Single object, three-quarter or side view, centred, fills most of the frame, transparent background, no text, no letters, no numbers.

The Neo-Andean Sun Kingdom builds light, high-altitude, sun-powered weapons: every vehicle and gun carries folding golden solar panels shaped like sun rays or condor feathers, frames are lightweight alloy over carved stone-like ceramic plates with Inca stepped (chakana) geometry, and machines are tall, narrow and long-legged to climb Andean slopes. Woven textile patterns (aguayo stripes) decorate armour, turquoise crystal lenses replace glass, and many weapons use gravity or wind (gliders, slings, rails). Palette: gold, turquoise, terracotta red, alpaca white and stone grey. Single object, three-quarter or side view, centred, fills most of the frame, transparent background, no text, no letters, no numbers.

Keep the faction materials, colours and silhouette family; only the technology level changes, like Infantry Equipment I, II, III in Hearts of Iron IV. FACTION DESIGN PHILOSOPHY - THE LEGION REBORN. The Neo-Roman Empire builds massive, imposing, ceremonial war machines that translate Roman legion equipment into 2100 technology: huge curved scutum-like shield plates, segmented lorica armour bands, crested helmet-like turrets with red plumes of cables or fins, eagle (aquila) standards and laurel wreaths in gold, marble-white and crimson colours, rows of rivets like a phalanx. Weapons are heavy, slow, overbuilt and theatrical; projectiles and shapes recall the pilum, the gladius, the ballista and the testudo. Palette: crimson, imperial purple, polished gold, marble white, bronze. Weapons are heavy, slow, overbuilt and theatrical; projectiles and shapes recall the pilum, the gladius, the ballista and the testudo. Palette: crimson, imperial purple, polished gold, marble white, bronze. Single object, three-quarter or side view, centred, fills most of the frame, transparent background, no text, no letters, no numbers.

Show the technological progress so it is obviously far newer than generation 1: each generation is longer, lower and better armoured, with a bigger gun, more angled armour plates, then reactive armour and active-protection launchers, finally sleek composite armour with sensors and a remote turret. Keep the faction materials, colours and silhouette family; only the technology level changes, like Infantry Equipment I, II, III in Hearts of Iron IV. FACTION DESIGN PHILOSOPHY - HYDRAULIC MASS PRODUCTION. The Sino-Harmonious Directorate designs simple, standardized, rugged machines that can be produced by the million: boxy, symmetrical, heavy, with no decoration except regulation markings. Everything is water-cooled and hydraulically driven: thick pipes, pistons, radiators, pressure gauges, water tanks and dam-like sluice armour plates; heavy weapons use water-pressure or steam-assisted launchers. The look is orderly and identical, like a parade of equal units: concrete-grey and jade-green paint, stencilled geometric unit codes (no letters), small red star-like harmony emblems (a circle with flowing waves). Single object, three-quarter or side view, centred, fills most of the frame, transparent background, no text, no letters, no numbers.
transparent_background: true

FACTION DESIGN PHILOSOPHY - RUGGED, REPAIRABLE, PEOPLE'S TECHNOLOGY. The African Peoples' Federation builds tough, simple, modern machines that any village council can maintain: open frames, big wheels, huge ground clearance, exposed easy-to-replace parts, civilian and agricultural origins (tractor, truck and mining machinery DNA), solar and biofuel power, lots of external storage, water jerrycans and spare tyres. Armour is painted with bright kente-like geometric bands; mobility and range matter more than heavy armour. Palette: earth red, savanna ochre, deep green, bright yellow and black geometric patterns. It is the most advanced, final version of the same weapon line, with the SAME faction design language as generation 1 (arma_HSN_medium_plane.png): The Albatross Bomber: a long-winged twin-engine flying boat bomber: a boat hull fuselage in container-blue with a red anti-fouling belly, very long slender albatross wings, wingtip floats, portholes along the side, a cargo-door bomb bay, a compass-rose emblem on the tail. Show the technological progress so it is obviously far newer than generation 1: each generation goes from propellers to early jets, swept wings, then stealthy delta shapes and finally an unmanned drone-like airframe. Keep the faction materials, colours and silhouette family; only the technology level changes, like Infantry Equipment I, II, III in Hearts of Iron IV. FACTION DESIGN PHILOSOPHY - EVERYTHING COMES FROM THE SEA TRADE. The High Seas Market Nation converts merchant-marine technology into weapons: hulls are shipping containers and boat hulls, mounts are dock cranes and winches, armour is riveted ship plate with hazard stripes and anti-fouling red paint below the "waterline", and almost every land vehicle is amphibious with propellers, rudders, bilge pumps and life-raft canisters. Nautical details everywhere: portholes, cleats, coiled ropes, anchors, signal flags without letters, compass roses, brass fittings, container corner castings. Palette: petrol teal, rust orange, container blue and red, brass, white hull numbers as shapes. Single object, three-quarter or side view, centred, fills most of the frame, transparent background, no text, no letters, no numbers.

The look is orderly and identical, like a parade of equal units: concrete-grey and jade-green paint, stencilled geometric unit codes (no letters), small red star-like harmony emblems (a circle with flowing waves).
